===========================================================
 GUROBI MODEL FULL DUMP
   - VARIABLES (name, lb, ub, type)
   - OBJECTIVE (sense + expression + term-by-term)
   - CONSTRAINTS (name + LHS + sense + RHS + term-by-term LHS)
   - OPTIONAL: write .lp / .mps
===========================================================

Notes:
- In Gurobi, constraints are stored as:  LHS (<=,==,>=) RHS
- LHS is a linear expression (variables + coefficients)
- RHS is a constant (float)
- Gurobi canonicalizes equations by moving variable terms to LHS,
  so RHS is often 0.0 (this is normal).
===========================================================
